Implement Phase 8 (Inbox surface) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 8).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. PHASE 8 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `runs/<run-id>/inbox/` directory for external completion-signal protocol.
- Files dropped into the inbox by external processes (humans, scripts, other tools) signal that an attested step has completed.
- Inbox files validate into events on the next `next`/`status`/`ack` call.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, lifecycle status/next handlers.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Inbox files validate into events.
- Stale or malformed files are ignored.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Define inbox file format. A simple JSON shape:
   ```json
   {
     "step_id": "...",
     "decision": "approve" | "retry" | "abort",
     "evidence": { ... step-specific artifacts ... },
     "submitted_at": "ISO 8601",
     "submitted_by": "<external-system-name>"
   }
   ```

2. New module `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`:
   - `inbox_dir(run_dir) -> Path` — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`
   - `scan_inbox(run_dir) -> list[InboxEntry]` — reads, validates, returns parsed entries. Malformed files are logged and skipped, not raised.
   - `consume_inbox_entry(run_dir, entry) -> EventRecord` — validates the entry against the current step expectations, writes a hash-chained event, then moves the inbox file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<hash>` (or deletes — pick one and document it).

3. Hook into lifecycle handlers:
   - `artagents next`: scans inbox before computing next step. If the inbox has a valid entry for the current attested step, consume it (record event), then advance the cursor.
   - `artagents status`: scans inbox in read-only mode and surfaces "X inbox entries pending" in output.
   - `artagents ack`: not changed (ack is the explicit verb; inbox is the implicit signal channel).

4. Tests:
   - `tests/test_inbox_scan.py`: drops 3 valid + 1 malformed file, scan returns 3 valid entries and ignores the malformed one without raising.
   - `tests/test_inbox_consume.py`: full flow — attested step waits, drop a valid inbox entry, call `next`, assert event is recorded and cursor advances.
   - `tests/test_inbox_stale.py`: drop an entry referencing a step_id that doesn't match current step, assert it's ignored (logged, not consumed).

5. `runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md` template should mention the inbox surface for human/external operators.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 8 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies.
- Inbox is opt-in: presence of `inbox/` directory triggers scanning, absence skips it.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 8 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new inbox tests + status/next surface inbox entries appropriately.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 8 — Inbox Surface

## Overview
Add an opt-in file-based external completion-signal protocol under `runs/<run-id>/inbox/`. External processes (humans, scripts, other tools) drop JSON files into this directory. On the next `next`/`status` call, `inbox.py` scans, validates, and consumes valid entries by recording hash-chained events to `events.jsonl` — advancing the cursor as if an explicit `ack` had been issued. Malformed or stale files are logged and skipped without crashing. The inbox directory triggers scanning only when it exists; its absence is a no-op.

**Key constraints**: additive only, no new dependencies, Phase 8 scope only, existing tests must pass, opt-in by directory presence.

## Phase 1: Core Inbox Module

### Step 1: Create `artagents/core/task/inbox.py`
**Scope:** Medium (~120 lines)

Implement the three core functions:

1. **`inbox_dir(run_dir: Path) -> Path`** — returns `<run_dir>/inbox/`.

2. **`InboxEntry` dataclass** — parsed representation:
   ```python
   @dataclass(frozen=True)
   class InboxEntry:
       step_id: str
       decision: str  # "approve" | "retry" | "abort"
       evidence: dict[str, Any]
       submitted_at: str  # ISO 8601
       submitted_by: str
       file_path: Path  # original file that was parsed
   ```

3. **`scan_inbox(run_dir: Path) -> list[InboxEntry]`** — reads all `.json` files in `inbox_dir(run_dir)`, validates structure, logs and skips malformed files (no raise). Returns parsed entries.

4. **`consume_inbox_entry(run_dir: Path, entry: InboxEntry, *, plan, events_path: Path, slug: str, projects_root: Path | None = None) -> bool`** — validates the entry against the current step expectations using `peek_current_step`, constructs the appropriate incoming command string (matching the pattern in `_ack_approve`), calls `gate_command` to record the event, then moves the consumed file to `<run_dir>/inbox/.consumed/<original-filename>-<sha256-trunc8>`. Returns True on success, False if entry doesn't match current step (logged, not consumed).

5. **`consume_pending_inbox(run_dir: Path, *, plan, events_path: Path, slug: str, projects_root: Path | None = None) -> int`** — convenience wrapper: scans inbox, consumes each matching entry, returns count of consumed entries. This is the single call site for lifecycle handlers.

**File**: `artagents/core/task/inbox.py` (new)

### Step 2: Add `make_inbox_consumed_event` to `artagents/core/task/events.py`
**Scope:** Tiny (~10 lines)

Add a new event kind `inbox_consumed` to the events module so inbox consumption is recorded in the hash chain:
```python
def make_inbox_consumed_event(
    plan_step_path: tuple[str, ...],
    *,
    decision: str,
    submitted_by: str,
    submitted_at: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
```

**File**: `artagents/core/task/events.py` (append new function near line 330)

### Step 3: Export new symbols from `artagents/core/task/__init__.py`
**Scope:** Tiny (~5 lines)

Add `InboxEntry`, `consume_pending_inbox`, `inbox_dir`, `scan_inbox` to the module exports. Add `make_inbox_consumed_event` to the events exports.

**File**: `artagents/core/task/__init__.py`

## Phase 2: Lifecycle Hooks

### Step 4: Hook `cmd_next` to consume inbox before computing next step
**Scope:** Small (~8 lines)

In `artagents/core/task/lifecycle.py`, inside `cmd_next` (around line 400, after `events = read_events(events_path)` and before `peek = peek_current_step(...)`), add:
```python
# Phase 8: consume pending inbox entries before computing next step
run_dir = proj_root / "runs" / run_id
consumed = consume_pending_inbox(run_dir, plan=plan, events_path=events_path, slug=slug, projects_root=projects_root)
if consumed:
    events = read_events(events_path)  # re-read after consumption
```

**File**: `artagents/core/task/lifecycle.py` (insert after line 407)

### Step 5: Hook `cmd_status` to surface pending inbox count
**Scope:** Small (~8 lines)

In `artagents/core/task/lifecycle.py`, inside `cmd_status` (around line 278, after `events = read_events(events_path)`), add:
```python
# Phase 8: surface pending inbox entries
inbox_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "inbox"
if inbox_path.is_dir():
    pending = [p for p in inbox_path.iterdir() if p.suffix == ".json" and p.is_file()]
    if pending:
        print(f"inbox:     {len(pending)} pending entr{'ies' if len(pending) != 1 else 'y'}")
```

**File**: `artagents/core/task/lifecycle.py` (insert after line 278)

### Step 6: Update `AGENT.md` template to mention inbox surface
**Scope:** Tiny (~5 lines)

In `artagents/core/task/lifecycle.py`, update `_AGENT_MD_TEMPLATE` (line 53) to add a note about the inbox surface under the RECOVERY COMMANDS section:
```
EXTERNAL INBOX
- Drop a JSON file into runs/<run-id>/inbox/ to signal completion without
  using the CLI. See docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md §12 for the file format.
  The inbox is scanned on every `artagents next` and `artagents status` call.
```

**File**: `artagents/core/task/lifecycle.py` (append to `_AGENT_MD_TEMPLATE` after line 68)

## Phase 3: Tests

### Step 7: Test inbox scan (`tests/test_inbox_scan.py`)
**Scope:** Medium (~60 lines)

Create test file with:
- **`test_scan_inbox_returns_valid_entries_skips_malformed`**: drops 3 valid JSON files + 1 malformed (invalid JSON), calls `scan_inbox`, asserts 3 entries returned, malformed is skipped without raising.
- **`test_scan_inbox_returns_empty_when_dir_absent`**: no inbox dir, returns empty list.
- **`test_scan_inbox_validates_required_fields`**: drops files missing `step_id`, `decision`, etc.; each is skipped.

**File**: `tests/test_inbox_scan.py` (new)

### Step 8: Test inbox consume (`tests/test_inbox_consume.py`)
**Scope:** Medium (~80 lines)

Create test file with:
- **`test_consume_inbox_entry_advances_cursor`**: sets up an attested plan via `_activate_attested_plan`, drops a valid inbox file referencing the correct `step_id`, calls `consume_pending_inbox`, asserts an `inbox_consumed` + `step_attested` event was written and cursor advanced.
- **`test_consume_inbox_entry_moves_file_to_consumed`**: after consumption, original file is moved to `.consumed/` subdirectory.
- **`test_consume_inbox_approve_decision_works`**: decision=approve produces correct events.

**File**: `tests/test_inbox_consume.py` (new)

### Step 9: Test inbox stale entries (`tests/test_inbox_stale.py`)
**Scope:** Small (~40 lines)

Create test file with:
- **`test_stale_entry_referencing_wrong_step_is_ignored`**: setup plan with step `s1`, drop inbox file referencing `s2` (non-existent step), assert `consume_pending_inbox` returns 0 consumed, file left in inbox (not consumed).
- **`test_stale_entry_after_plan_advanced_is_ignored`**: advance past `s1`, drop inbox file for `s1`, assert it's ignored (stale).

**File**: `tests/test_inbox_stale.py` (new)

### Step 10: Test lifecycle surface (`tests/test_inbox_lifecycle.py`)
**Scope:** Small (~50 lines)

Create test file with:
- **`test_cmd_next_consumes_inbox_before_peek`**: integration test using the actual `cmd_next`/`cmd_status` CLI functions, asserts inbox entry is consumed and cursor advances.
- **`test_cmd_status_shows_pending_inbox_count`**: drops 2 inbox files, calls `cmd_status`, asserts output contains "inbox: 2 pending entries".

**File**: `tests/test_inbox_lifecycle.py` (new)

## Execution Order
1. **Step 1** (inbox.py module) — foundation, no dependencies on other changes.
2. **Step 2** (events.py addition) — required before Step 1 can call `append_event` with the new event kind.
3. **Step 3** (__init__.py exports) — after Steps 1-2.
4. **Step 7** (test_inbox_scan.py) — can run immediately after Step 1, validates the parser in isolation.
5. **Step 4** (hook cmd_next) — wires consumption into the lifecycle.
6. **Step 5** (hook cmd_status) — wires inbox count into status output.
7. **Step 6** (AGENT.md template) — documentation, no code impact.
8. **Steps 8-10** (consume, stale, lifecycle tests) — after all hooks are in place.

## Validation Order
1. Run `pytest tests/test_inbox_scan.py -v` after Step 1+2+3.
2. Run `pytest tests/test_inbox_consume.py tests/test_inbox_stale.py -v` after Step 4.
3. Run `pytest tests/test_inbox_lifecycle.py -v` after Step 5.
4. Run `pytest tests/ -v` — full suite must pass, no regressions.

76	def _resolve_packs_root(packs_root: Optional[Path]) -> Path:
77	    if packs_root is not None:
78	        return Path(packs_root)
79	    from artagents.orchestrate.compile import DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT
80	    return DEFAULT_PACKS_ROOT
81	
82	
83	def _qualified_split(qualified_id: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
84	    if not isinstance(qualified_id, str) or "." not in qualified_id:
85	        raise ValueError(
86	            f"orchestrator id {qualified_id!r} must be '<pack>.<name>'"
87	        )
88	    pack, _, name = qualified_id.partition(".")
89	    if not pack or not name or "." in name:
90	        raise ValueError(
91	            f"orchestrator id {qualified_id!r} must be exactly '<pack>.<name>'"
92	        )
93	    return pack, name
94	
95	
96	def _generate_run_id() -> str:
97	    stamp = datetime.now(UTC).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")
98	    return f"run-{stamp}-{secrets.token_hex(4)}"
99

101	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
102	# cmd_start
103	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
104	
105	
106	def cmd_start(
107	    argv: Sequence[str],
108	    *,
109	    packs_root: Optional[Path] = None,
110	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
111	) -> int:
112	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="artagents start", add_help=True)
113	    parser.add_argument("orchestrator_id", help="qualified id <pack>.<name>")
114	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
115	    parser.add_argument("--name", default=None, help="optional run id (slug-validated)")
116	    try:
117	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
118	    except SystemExit as exc:
119	        return int(exc.code or 2)
120	
121	    try:
122	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
123	    except Exception as exc:
124	        _print_err(f"start: {exc}")
125	        return 1
126	
127	    try:
128	        pack, name = _qualified_split(args.orchestrator_id)
129	    except ValueError as exc:
130	        _print_err(f"start: {exc}")
131	        return 1
132	
133	    if read_active_run(slug, root=projects_root) is not None:
134	        _print_err(
135	            f"start: active run already exists for project {slug!r}; "
136	            f"recovery: artagents abort --project {slug}"
137	        )
138	        return 1
139	
140	    packs = _resolve_packs_root(packs_root)
141	    build_path = packs / pack / "build" / f"{name}.json"
142	    if not build_path.is_file():
143	        _print_err(
144	            f"start: compiled plan not found at {build_path}; "
145	            f"recovery: artagents author compile {args.orchestrator_id}"
146	        )
147	        return 1
148	
149	    try:
150	        compiled_payload = json.loads(build_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
151	    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
152	        _print_err(f"start: failed to read {build_path}: {exc}")
153	        return 1
154	
155	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
156	    proj_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
157	    plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
158	    write_json_atomic(plan_path, compiled_payload)
159	
160	    try:
161	        load_plan(plan_path)
162	    except Exception as exc:
163	        _print_err(f"start: compiled plan failed validation: {exc}")
164	        return 1
165	
166	    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)
167	
168	    if args.name is not None:
169	        try:
170	            run_id = validate_run_id(args.name)
171	        except Exception as exc:
172	            _print_err(f"start: --name {exc}")
173	            return 1
174	    else:
175	        run_id = _generate_run_id()
176	
177	    run_dir = proj_root / "runs" / run_id
178	    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
179	
180	    write_active_run(slug, run_id=run_id, plan_hash=plan_hash, root=projects_root)
181	
182	    events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"
183	    actor = task_actor_env()
184	    append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event(run_id, plan_hash, actor=actor))
185	
186	    agent_md = _AGENT_MD_TEMPLATE.format(
187	        preamble=PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE,
188	        qualified_id=args.orchestrator_id,
189	        run_id=run_id,
190	        slug=slug,
191	    )
192	    (run_dir / "AGENT.md").write_text(agent_md, encoding="utf-8")
193	
194	    print(f"started {args.orchestrator_id}")
195	    print(f"  project:   {slug}")
196	    print(f"  run-id:    {run_id}")
197	    print(f"  plan-hash: {plan_hash}")
198	    return 0
199

206	def cmd_abort(
207	    argv: Sequence[str],
208	    *,
209	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
210	) -> int:
211	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="artagents abort", add_help=True)
212	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
213	    parser.add_argument("--reason", default=None, help="optional human-readable reason")
214	    try:
215	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
216	    except SystemExit as exc:
217	        return int(exc.code or 2)
218	
219	    try:
220	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
221	    except Exception as exc:
222	        _print_err(f"abort: {exc}")
223	        return 1
224	
225	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)
226	    if active_run is None:
227	        # Idempotent — Phase 6 Stop-hook may invoke abort defensively.
228	        return 0
229	
230	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
231	    events_path = (
232	        project_dir(slug, root=projects_root) / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
233	    )
234	    append_event(events_path, make_run_aborted_event(run_id, reason=args.reason))
235	    clear_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)
236	    print(f"aborted {run_id}")
237	    return 0

281	    )

284	    print(f"plan-hash: {plan_hash}")
285	    if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None:
286	        print("current:   <run exhausted>")
287	    else:
288	        path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)
289	        kind = "code" if isinstance(peek.step, CodeStep) else (
290	            "attested" if isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep) else "nested"
291	        )
292	        suffix = ""
293	        if peek.iteration is not None:
294	            suffix += f"  iter={peek.iteration}"
295	        if peek.item_id is not None:
296	            suffix += f"  item={peek.item_id}"
297	        print(f"current:   {path_str} [{kind}]{suffix}")
298	        if peek.step.produces:
299	            names = ", ".join(p.name for p in peek.step.produces)
300	            print(f"produces:  {names}")
301	
302	    print("recent events:")
303	    for ev in events[-5:]:
304	        kind = ev.get("kind", "?")
305	        ts = ev.get("ts", "")
306	        plan_step_id = ev.get("plan_step_id")
307	        if not isinstance(plan_step_id, str):
308	            path = ev.get("plan_step_path")
309	            plan_step_id = "/".join(path) if isinstance(path, list) else ""
310	        print(f"  {ts}  {kind}  {plan_step_id}")
311	    return 0

314	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
315	# cmd_next
316	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
317	
318	
319	def _format_ack_template(
320	    *, path_str: str, slug: str, ack_kind: str, has_repeat_for_each: bool
321	) -> str:
322	    identity = "--agent <id>" if ack_kind == "agent" else "--actor <name>"
323	    base = (
324	        f"artagents ack {path_str} --project {slug} --decision approve "
325	        f"{identity} [--evidence path ...]"
326	    )
327	    if has_repeat_for_each:
328	        base += " [--item <id>]"
329	    return base
330	
331	
332	def _find_step_by_path(plan, path_tuple):
333	    """Walk a TaskPlan to find the step at ``path_tuple`` (descending NestedStep
334	    children). Returns the step or None if the path does not resolve.
335	    """
336	    if not path_tuple:
337	        return None
338	    steps = plan.steps
339	    for segment in path_tuple[:-1]:
340	        match = next((s for s in steps if s.id == segment), None)
341	        if match is None or not isinstance(match, NestedStep):
342	            return None
343	        steps = match.plan.steps
344	    return next((s for s in steps if s.id == path_tuple[-1]), None)
345	
346	
347	def _completed_items_from_events(events, host_path):
348	    """Return the set of item ids that have a completed/attested event under
349	    ``host_path``. ``host_path`` is the STEP_PATH_SEP-joined string form.
350	    """
351	    path_list = host_path.split(STEP_PATH_SEP) if host_path else []
352	    completed: set[str] = set()
353	    for ev in events:
354	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
355	            continue
356	        kind = ev.get("kind")
357	        if kind not in ("item_completed", "item_attested"):
358	            continue
359	        if ev.get("plan_step_path") != path_list:
360	            continue
361	        item_id = ev.get("item_id")
362	        if isinstance(item_id, str):
363	            completed.add(item_id)
364	    return completed
365	
366	
367	def cmd_next(
368	    argv: Sequence[str],
369	    *,
370	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
371	) -> int:
372	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="artagents next", add_help=True)
373	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
374	    try:
375	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
376	    except SystemExit as exc:
377	        return int(exc.code or 2)
378	
379	    try:
380	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
381	    except Exception as exc:
382	        # Preamble must precede every operator-facing message (SD-023).
383	        print(PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE)
384	        print()
385	        _print_err(f"next: {exc}")
386	        return 1
387	
388	    # Always print preamble first, verbatim, every call (SD-023) — even on
389	    # error / exhausted paths so Stop-hook context re-injection is consistent.
390	    print(PROHIBITION_PREAMBLE)
391	    print()
392	
393	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)
394	    if active_run is None:
395	        _print_err(
396	            f"next: no active run for project {slug!r}; "
397	            f"recovery: artagents start <orchestrator-id> --project {slug}"
398	        )
399	        return 1
400	
401	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
402	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
403	    plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
404	    events_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
405	
406	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
407	    events = read_events(events_path)
408	    peek = peek_current_step(
409	        plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id
410	    )
411	
412	    if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None:
413	        print("run complete")
414	        print(f"recovery: artagents abort --project {slug}")
415	        return 0
416	
417	    path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)
418	
419	    if isinstance(peek.step, CodeStep):
420	        print(f"run: {peek.step.command}")
421	        print(
422	            "(rerun the same command if it failed; the gate detects re-entry "
423	            "and skips a duplicate step_dispatched event.)"
424	        )
425	    elif isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep):
426	        print(peek.step.instructions)
427	        print()
428	        # peek.step.repeat is None when the leaf is the body of a repeat
429	        # frame (the body is a clone with repeat stripped) — peek.item_id
430	        # being set is the reliable signal that we're inside a for_each
431	        # host. Fall back to looking up the host in the plan when item_id
432	        # is None to handle a top-level for_each that hasn't dispatched yet.
433	        host_has_for_each = peek.item_id is not None
434	        if not host_has_for_each:
435	            host_step = _find_step_by_path(plan, peek.path_tuple)
436	            if host_step is not None and isinstance(
437	                getattr(host_step, "repeat", None), RepeatForEach
438	            ):
439	                host_has_for_each = True
440	        print(
441	            _format_ack_template(
442	                path_str=path_str,
443	                slug=slug,
444	                ack_kind=peek.step.ack.kind,
445	                has_repeat_for_each=host_has_for_each,
446	            )
447	        )
448	    else:
449	        # Defensive: peek_current_step should never surface a NestedStep.
450	        _print_err(f"next: unexpected step kind {type(peek.step).__name__}")
451	        return 1
452	
453	    # Iteration ledger: at peek.iteration == N (>=2), read iteration N-1's
454	    # cumulative feedback.json (written by write_iteration_feedback).
455	    if peek.iteration is not None and peek.iteration >= 2:
456	        prev_iter = peek.iteration - 1
457	        try:
458	            prev_dir = step_dir_for_path(
459	                slug,
460	                run_id,
461	                peek.path_tuple,
462	                iteration=prev_iter,
463	                root=projects_root,
464	            )
465	        except Exception:
466	            prev_dir = None
467	        if prev_dir is not None:
468	            feedback_path = prev_dir / "feedback.json"
469	            if feedback_path.is_file():
470	                try:
471	                    payload = json.loads(feedback_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
472	                except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
473	                    payload = None
474	                if isinstance(payload, list):
475	                    print()
476	                    print(f"feedback ledger (through iteration {prev_iter}):")
477	                    for idx, entry in enumerate(payload, start=1):
478	                        print(f"  [{idx}] {entry}")
479	
480	    # for_each item ledger: when peek.item_id is set, the leaf is the body
481	    # step inside a for_each frame; peek.path_tuple matches the host path
482	    # because _make_item_frame uses path_prefix = parent_prefix and the body
483	    # carries the host's id. Look the host step up directly from the plan
484	    # (peek does not persist for_each_expanded to events.jsonl, so
485	    # derive_cursor's for_each_progress would be empty here).
486	    if peek.item_id is not None:
487	        host_path = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)
488	        host_step = _find_step_by_path(plan, peek.path_tuple)
489	        items: list[str] = []
490	        if host_step is not None and isinstance(
491	            getattr(host_step, "repeat", None), RepeatForEach
492	        ):
493	            host_for_each: RepeatForEach = host_step.repeat  # type: ignore[assignment]
494	            if host_for_each.items_source == "static":
495	                items = list(host_for_each.items)
496	            # Dynamic items source — items are resolved at gate dispatch from
497	            # a sibling produces JSON file. peek shares the same resolution
498	            # path; if events.jsonl has a for_each_expanded event for this
499	            # host (because dispatch ran earlier) we can recover items from
500	            # there instead.
501	        if not items:
502	            for ev in events:
503	                if (
504	                    isinstance(ev, dict)
505	                    and ev.get("kind") == "for_each_expanded"
506	                    and ev.get("plan_step_path") == list(peek.path_tuple)
507	                ):
508	                    raw = ev.get("item_ids") or []
509	                    if isinstance(raw, list):
510	                        items = [str(x) for x in raw]
511	                    break
512	        completed = _completed_items_from_events(events, host_path)
513	        if items:
514	            print()
515	            print(f"for_each items (host {host_path}):")
516	            for item in items:
517	                marker = "x" if item in completed else " "
518	                star = "  <- next" if item == peek.item_id else ""
519	                print(f"  [{marker}] {item}{star}")
520	
521	    return 0
522

524	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
525	# cmd_runs_ls
526	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
527	
528	
529	def _summarize_run_dir(run_dir: Path) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
530	    """Return (status, last_event_kind, last_ts) for a run directory.
531	
532	    Per FLAG-P5-006: only ``aborted`` vs ``in-progress`` are reliably
533	    distinguishable in V1. A naturally completed plan still leaves
534	    ``active_run.json`` in place, so the "complete" bucket is mostly
535	    unobservable.
536	    """
537	    events_path = run_dir / "events.jsonl"
538	    if not events_path.is_file():
539	        return "in-progress", "", ""
540	    events = read_events(events_path)
541	    if not events:
542	        return "in-progress", "", ""
543	    last = events[-1]
544	    last_kind = str(last.get("kind", ""))
545	    last_ts = str(last.get("ts", ""))
546	    status = "aborted" if last_kind == "run_aborted" else "in-progress"
547	    return status, last_kind, last_ts
548	
549	
550	def cmd_runs_ls(
551	    argv: Sequence[str],
552	    *,
553	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
554	) -> int:
555	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="artagents runs ls", add_help=True)
556	    parser.add_argument("--project", default=None, help="optional project slug filter")
557	    try:
558	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
559	    except SystemExit as exc:
560	        return int(exc.code or 2)
561	
562	    if args.project is not None:
563	        try:
564	            slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
565	        except Exception as exc:
566	            _print_err(f"runs ls: {exc}")
567	            return 1
568	        project_dirs = [project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)]
569	    else:
570	        root = resolve_projects_root(projects_root)
571	        if not root.is_dir():
572	            return 0
573	        project_dirs = sorted(p for p in root.iterdir() if p.is_dir())
574	
575	    rows: list[tuple[str, str, str, str, str]] = []
576	    for proj in project_dirs:
577	        runs_root = proj / "runs"
578	        if not runs_root.is_dir():
579	            continue
580	        for run_dir in sorted(p for p in runs_root.iterdir() if p.is_dir()):
581	            status, last_kind, last_ts = _summarize_run_dir(run_dir)
582	            rows.append((proj.name, run_dir.name, status, last_kind, last_ts))
583	
584	    for slug, run_id, status, last_kind, last_ts in rows:
585	        print(f"{slug}\t{run_id}\t{status}\t{last_kind}\t{last_ts}")
586	    return 0
587

1	"""``artagents ack`` lifecycle verb (Phase 5 T9).
2	
3	Split out of ``lifecycle.py`` to keep both modules under the ~600-line size
4	budget. Implements the ack decision matrix per the Phase 5 brief:
5	
6	- ``approve`` (attested cursor): synthesizes the gate-bound incoming command
7	  as ``step.command + identity/evidence/item tokens`` (NOT ``ack --step ...``)
8	  so ``match_attested_command`` can strip identity tokens and compare the
9	  literal remainder to ``step.command`` for authored commands like
10	  ``review.sh``. Calls ``gate_command`` + ``record_dispatch_complete``.
11	  (FLAG-P5-001.)
12	- ``approve`` on a CodeStep cursor: rejected. Code steps advance via the
13	  printed argv, not via ``ack``.
14	- ``retry``: only valid on an AttestedStep cursor whose latest event for
15	  the path is ``produces_check_failed``. Calls ``validate_attested_identity``
16	  BEFORE mutating events, then appends ``cursor_rewind`` so the next
17	  ``next`` re-dispatches. (FLAG-P5-002.)
18	- ``iterate``: only valid on an AttestedStep cursor whose host has
19	  ``repeat.until.condition == 'user_approves'``. Requires non-empty
20	  ``--feedback``. Calls ``validate_attested_identity`` BEFORE mutating
21	  events, then ``write_iteration_feedback`` (cumulative ledger) and
22	  appends ``iteration_failed`` so the next ``next`` enters iteration N+1.
23	  (FLAG-P5-002.)
24	- ``abort``: administrative — delegates to ``cmd_abort`` and skips identity
25	  validation entirely.
26	"""
27	
28	from __future__ import annotations
29	
30	import argparse
31	import shlex
32	import sys
33	from pathlib import Path
34	from typing import Optional, Sequence
35	
36	from artagents.core.project.paths import project_dir, validate_project_slug
37	from artagents.core.task.active_run import read_active_run
38	from artagents.core.task.events import (
39	    append_event,
40	    make_cursor_rewind_event,
41	    make_iteration_failed_event,
42	    read_events,
43	)
44	from artagents.core.task.gate import (
45	    AttestedArgs,
46	    GateDecision,
47	    TaskRunGateError,
48	    gate_command,
49	    peek_current_step,

53	)
54	from artagents.core.task.plan import (
55	    STEP_PATH_SEP,
56	    AttestedStep,
57	    CodeStep,
58	    NestedStep,
59	    RepeatUntil,
60	    load_plan,
61	)
62	
63	
64	def _print_err(msg: str) -> None:
65	    print(msg, file=sys.stderr)
66	
67	
68	def _find_step_by_path(plan, path_tuple):
69	    """Walk a TaskPlan to find the step at ``path_tuple``."""
70	    if not path_tuple:
71	        return None
72	    steps = plan.steps
73	    for segment in path_tuple[:-1]:
74	        match = next((s for s in steps if s.id == segment), None)
75	        if match is None or not isinstance(match, NestedStep):
76	            return None
77	        steps = match.plan.steps
78	    return next((s for s in steps if s.id == path_tuple[-1]), None)
79	
80	
81	def _latest_event_for_path(events, path_tuple):
82	    path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(path_tuple)
83	    path_list = list(path_tuple)
84	    for ev in reversed(events):
85	        if not isinstance(ev, dict):
86	            continue
87	        if ev.get("plan_step_id") == path_str:
88	            return ev
89	        if ev.get("plan_step_path") == path_list:
90	            return ev
91	    return None
92	
93	
94	def _run_started_actor(events) -> Optional[str]:
95	    for ev in events:
96	        if isinstance(ev, dict) and ev.get("kind") == "run_started":
97	            actor = ev.get("actor")
98	            return actor if isinstance(actor, str) else None
99	    return None
100	
101	
102	def cmd_ack(
103	    argv: Sequence[str],
104	    *,
105	    projects_root: Optional[Path] = None,
106	) -> int:
107	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="artagents ack", add_help=True)
108	    parser.add_argument("step", help="STEP_PATH_SEP-joined plan step path (e.g. 'review' or 'outer/inner')")
109	    parser.add_argument("--project", required=True, help="project slug")
110	    parser.add_argument(
111	        "--decision",
112	        required=True,
113	        choices=["approve", "retry", "iterate", "abort"],
114	        help="ack decision",
115	    )
116	    parser.add_argument(
117	        "--evidence",
118	        action="append",
119	        default=[],
120	        help="repeatable; evidence path or sentinel",
121	    )
122	    identity = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
123	    identity.add_argument("--agent", default=None, help="agent id (mutually exclusive with --actor)")
124	    identity.add_argument("--actor", default=None, help="actor name (mutually exclusive with --agent)")
125	    parser.add_argument("--feedback", default=None, help="iterate feedback (required for --decision=iterate)")
126	    parser.add_argument("--item", default=None, help="for_each item id")
127	    try:
128	        args = parser.parse_args(list(argv))
129	    except SystemExit as exc:
130	        return int(exc.code or 2)
131	
132	    try:
133	        slug = validate_project_slug(args.project)
134	    except Exception as exc:
135	        _print_err(f"ack: {exc}")
136	        return 1
137	
138	    # abort decision is administrative: delegate to cmd_abort, no identity required.
139	    if args.decision == "abort":
140	        from artagents.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_abort
141	        return cmd_abort(["--project", slug], projects_root=projects_root)
142	
143	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=projects_root)
144	    if active_run is None:
145	        _print_err(
146	            f"ack: no active run for project {slug!r}; "
147	            f"recovery: artagents start <orchestrator-id> --project {slug}"
148	        )
149	        return 1
150	
151	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
152	    proj_root = project_dir(slug, root=projects_root)
153	    plan_path = proj_root / "plan.json"
154	    events_path = proj_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
155	
156	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
157	    events = read_events(events_path)
158	    peek = peek_current_step(
159	        plan, events, slug, project_root=proj_root, run_id=run_id
160	    )
161	    if peek.exhausted or peek.step is None:
162	        _print_err(
163	            f"ack: run is exhausted; recovery: artagents abort --project {slug}"
164	        )
165	        return 1
166	
167	    expected_path = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)
168	    if args.step != expected_path:
169	        _print_err(
170	            f"ack: step path {args.step!r} does not match cursor {expected_path!r}; "
171	            f"run `artagents next --project {slug}` to see the current step"
172	        )
173	        return 1
174	
175	    if args.decision == "approve":
176	        return _ack_approve(args, slug, peek, projects_root, proj_root)
177	    if args.decision == "retry":
178	        return _ack_retry(
179	            args, slug, peek, plan, events, events_path, run_id, proj_root
180	        )
181	    if args.decision == "iterate":
182	        return _ack_iterate(
183	            args, slug, peek, plan, events, events_path, run_id, proj_root
184	        )
185	    # argparse choices=... already constrains this; defensive only.
186	    _print_err(f"ack: unknown decision {args.decision!r}")
187	    return 1
188	
189	
190	def _ack_approve(args, slug, peek, projects_root, proj_root) -> int:
191	    if isinstance(peek.step, CodeStep):
192	        _print_err(
193	            "ack: approve is invalid for code steps. code steps advance via "
194	            f"subprocess; just run the printed command (artagents next --project {slug})."
195	        )
196	        return 1
197	    if not isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep):
198	        _print_err(
199	            f"ack: cannot approve step kind {type(peek.step).__name__}"
200	        )
201	        return 1
202	
203	    # FLAG-P5-001: synthesize the incoming command as step.command +
204	    # identity/evidence/item tokens, NOT 'ack --step ...'.
205	    parts: list[str] = [peek.step.command]
206	    if args.agent:
207	        parts += ["--agent", args.agent]
208	    if args.actor:
209	        parts += ["--actor", args.actor]
210	    for ev in args.evidence:
211	        parts += ["--evidence", ev]
212	    if args.item:
213	        parts += ["--item", args.item]
214	    incoming = " ".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in parts)
215	
216	    try:
217	        decision = gate_command(slug, incoming, [], root=projects_root)
218	    except TaskRunGateError as exc:
219	        _print_err(f"ack: {exc.reason}; recovery: {exc.recovery}")
220	        return 1
221	
222	    # Attested step is "complete" at attestation; the gate already wrote
223	    # step_attested + ran inline produces checks. record_dispatch_complete
224	    # is a no-op for attested steps but we call it for symmetry with code
225	    # dispatch and to keep the post-dispatch surface consistent.
226	    record_dispatch_complete(decision, 0)
227	    print(f"acknowledged {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)}")
228	    return 0
229	
230	
231	def _ack_retry(args, slug, peek, plan, events, events_path, run_id, proj_root) -> int:
232	    if not isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep):
233	        _print_err(
234	            "ack: retry is only valid on attested steps. Code steps "
235	            "redispatch implicitly when you re-run the printed argv."
236	        )
237	        return 1
238	
239	    # FLAG-P5-002: validate identity BEFORE mutating events.
240	    attested_args = AttestedArgs(
241	        agent=args.agent,
242	        actor=args.actor,
243	        evidence=tuple(args.evidence),
244	        item=args.item,
245	    )
246	    try:
247	        validate_attested_identity(
248	            slug=slug,
249	            step=peek.step,
250	            args=attested_args,
251	            run_started_actor=_run_started_actor(events),
252	        )
253	    except TaskRunGateError as exc:
254	        _print_err(f"ack retry: {exc.reason}; recovery: {exc.recovery}")
255	        return 1
256	
257	    latest = _latest_event_for_path(events, peek.path_tuple)
258	    if not isinstance(latest, dict) or latest.get("kind") != "produces_check_failed":
259	        _print_err(
260	            "ack retry: only valid after a verifier failure (the latest event "
261	            f"for {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)} must be "
262	            "produces_check_failed)."
263	        )
264	        return 1
265	
266	    append_event(
267	        events_path,
268	        make_cursor_rewind_event(peek.path_tuple, reason="ack retry"),
269	    )
270	    print(f"retry queued for {STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)}")
271	    return 0
272	
273	
274	def _ack_iterate(args, slug, peek, plan, events, events_path, run_id, proj_root) -> int:
275	    if not isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep):
276	        _print_err("ack: iterate is only valid on attested steps")
277	        return 1
278	    if not args.feedback or not args.feedback.strip():
279	        _print_err("ack iterate: --feedback is required and must be non-empty")
280	        return 1
281	
282	    # FLAG-P5-002: validate identity BEFORE mutating events.
283	    attested_args = AttestedArgs(
284	        agent=args.agent,
285	        actor=args.actor,
286	        evidence=tuple(args.evidence),
287	        item=args.item,
288	    )
289	    try:
290	        validate_attested_identity(
291	            slug=slug,
292	            step=peek.step,
293	            args=attested_args,
294	            run_started_actor=_run_started_actor(events),
295	        )
296	    except TaskRunGateError as exc:
297	        _print_err(f"ack iterate: {exc.reason}; recovery: {exc.recovery}")
298	        return 1
299	
300	    # Find the host step in the plan (peek.step has repeat stripped because
301	    # it is the body of an iteration frame). peek.path_tuple == host path
302	    # because _make_iteration_frame uses path_prefix = parent_prefix.
303	    host = _find_step_by_path(plan, peek.path_tuple)
304	    if (
305	        host is None
306	        or not isinstance(getattr(host, "repeat", None), RepeatUntil)
307	        or host.repeat.condition != "user_approves"  # type: ignore[union-attr]
308	    ):
309	        condition = (
310	            host.repeat.condition  # type: ignore[union-attr]
311	            if host is not None and isinstance(getattr(host, "repeat", None), RepeatUntil)
312	            else "<no repeat>"
313	        )
314	        _print_err(
315	            "ack iterate: only valid for repeat.until.condition='user_approves' "
316	            f"(host condition={condition!r})"
317	        )
318	        return 1
319	    if peek.iteration is None:
320	        _print_err("ack iterate: cursor is not inside an iteration frame")
321	        return 1
322	
323	    decision = GateDecision(
324	        active=True,
325	        run_id=run_id,
326	        slug=slug,
327	        project_root=proj_root,
328	        plan_step_path=peek.path_tuple,
329	        iteration=peek.iteration,
330	        events_path=events_path,
331	    )
332	    write_iteration_feedback(decision, args.feedback)
333	    append_event(
334	        events_path,
335	        make_iteration_failed_event(
336	            peek.path_tuple, peek.iteration, reason="iterate_feedback"
337	        ),
338	    )
339	    print(
340	        f"iteration {peek.iteration} marked failed; feedback recorded for "
341	        f"{STEP_PATH_SEP.join(peek.path_tuple)}"
342	    )
343	    return 0
344	
345	
346	__all__ = ["cmd_ack"]
347

1	"""Hash-chained task-run event log."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import errno
6	import hashlib
7	import json
8	import os
9	from datetime import UTC, datetime
10	from pathlib import Path
11	from typing import Any
12	
13	ZERO_HASH = "sha256:" + "0" * 64
14	
15	
16	class EventLogError(RuntimeError):
17	    """Raised when a task event log cannot be read or written."""
18	
19	
20	def canonical_event_json(event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
21	    payload = {key: value for key, value in event.items() if key != "hash"}
22	    return json.dumps(payload, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False)
23	
24	
25	def append_event(path: str | Path, event: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
26	    events_path = Path(path)
27	    ok, _last_index, error = verify_chain(events_path)
28	    if not ok:
29	        raise EventLogError(error or f"invalid event hash chain in {events_path}")
30	
31	    previous_events = read_events(events_path)
32	    prev_hash = previous_events[-1]["hash"] if previous_events else ZERO_HASH
33	
34	    stored = dict(event)
35	    stored.pop("hash", None)
36	    stored["hash"] = _event_hash(prev_hash, stored)
37	
38	    events_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
39	    created = not events_path.exists()
40	    try:
41	        with events_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
42	            handle.write(json.dumps(stored, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False))
43	            handle.write("\n")
44	            handle.flush()
45	            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
46	        if created:
47	            _fsync_dir(events_path.parent)
48	    except OSError as exc:
49	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to append event to {events_path}: {exc}") from exc
50	    return stored
51	
52	
53	def verify_chain(path: str | Path) -> tuple[bool, int, str | None]:
54	    events_path = Path(path)
55	    try:
56	        handle = events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8")
57	    except FileNotFoundError:
58	        return True, -1, None
59	    except OSError as exc:
60	        return False, -1, f"failed to read {events_path}: {exc}"
61	
62	    prev_hash = ZERO_HASH
63	    last_index = -1
64	    with handle:
65	        for index, line in enumerate(handle):
66	            if not line.endswith("\n"):
67	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not newline-terminated"
68	            raw = line[:-1]
69	            if not raw:
70	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is empty"
71	            try:
72	                event = json.loads(raw)
73	            except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
74	                return False, index, f"invalid JSON on event log line {index + 1}: {exc.msg}"
75	            if not isinstance(event, dict):
76	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is not an object"
77	            stored_hash = event.get("hash")
78	            if not isinstance(stored_hash, str):
79	                return False, index, f"event log line {index + 1} is missing hash"
80	            expected_hash = _event_hash(prev_hash, event)
81	            if stored_hash != expected_hash:
82	                return (
83	                    False,
84	                    index,
85	                    f"event log line {index + 1} hash mismatch: expected {expected_hash}, got {stored_hash}",
86	                )
87	            prev_hash = stored_hash
88	            last_index = index
89	    return True, last_index, None
90	
91	
92	def read_events(path: str | Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
93	    events_path = Path(path)
94	    try:
95	        with events_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
96	            return [json.loads(line) for line in handle]
97	    except FileNotFoundError:
98	        return []
99	    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
100	        raise EventLogError(f"invalid JSON in {events_path}: {exc.msg}") from exc
101	    except OSError as exc:
102	        raise EventLogError(f"failed to read {events_path}: {exc}") from exc

324	) -> dict[str, Any]:
325	    return {
326	        "kind": "cursor_rewind",
327	        "plan_step_path": list(plan_step_path),
328	        "reason": reason,
329	        "ts": _utc_now_iso(),
330	    }
331	
332	
333	def _event_hash(prev_hash: str, event: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
334	    digest = hashlib.sha256((prev_hash + canonical_event_json(event)).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
335	    return f"sha256:{digest}"
336	
337	
338	def _utc_now_iso() -> str:
339	    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat().replace("+00:00", "Z")
340	
341	
342	def _fsync_dir(path: Path) -> None:
343	    flags = getattr(os, "O_DIRECTORY", 0) | os.O_RDONLY
344	    fd: int | None = None
345	    try:
346	        fd = os.open(path, flags)
347	        os.fsync(fd)
348	    except OSError as exc:
349	        if exc.errno not in {errno.EINVAL, errno.ENOTSUP, errno.EBADF}:
350	            raise
351	    finally:
352	        if fd is not None:
353	            os.close(fd)
354

454	def peek_current_step(
455	    plan: TaskPlan,
456	    events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
457	    slug: str,
458	    *,
459	    project_root: Path,
460	    run_id: str,
461	) -> PeekResult:
462	    """Walk the cursor exactly the way the gate would, but with a list-capturing
463	    ``append_fn`` so ``events.jsonl`` is never mutated.
464	
465	    Shares ``_auto_traverse_to_leaf`` with ``gate_command`` so peek and dispatch
466	    cannot drift on iteration / for_each / nested transitions (FLAG-P5-003).
467	    The captured events are kept in ``events_view`` so prior-event scans inside
468	    the auto-traverse helpers (``_count_iteration_failed``, the
469	    ``for_each_expanded`` lookup) see them; after every append we let the helper
470	    proceed and re-evaluate the cursor — which is equivalent to recomputing
471	    ``derive_cursor(plan, events + captured, slug=slug)`` because the helper
472	    performs the same frame mutations that ``derive_cursor`` would on replay.
473	    """
474	    cursor = derive_cursor(plan, events, slug=slug)
475	    if cursor.pinned_failure is not None or cursor.at_root_done:
476	        return PeekResult(step=None, path_tuple=(), iteration=None, item_id=None, exhausted=True)
477	
478	    events_view = list(events)
479	    captured: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
480	
481	    def _peek_append(ev: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
482	        captured.append(ev)
483	        events_view.append(ev)
484	
485	    leaf = _auto_traverse_to_leaf(
486	        slug=slug,
487	        cursor=cursor,
488	        events_view=events_view,
489	        incoming_command="",
490	        project_root=project_root,
491	        run_id=run_id,
492	        append_fn=_peek_append,
493	        raise_on_exhausted=False,
494	    )
495	    if leaf is None:
496	        return PeekResult(step=None, path_tuple=(), iteration=None, item_id=None, exhausted=True)
497	    step, path_tuple = leaf
498	    top = cursor.frames[-1]
499	    iteration = top.iteration if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
500	    item_id = top.item_id if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
501	    return PeekResult(
502	        step=step,
503	        path_tuple=path_tuple,
504	        iteration=iteration,
505	        item_id=item_id,
506	        exhausted=False,
507	    )
508	
509	
510	def gate_command(
511	    slug: str,
512	    command: str,
513	    argv: Sequence[str],
514	    *,
515	    root: str | Path | None = None,
516	    reentry: bool = False,
517	) -> GateDecision:
518	    active_run = read_active_run(slug, root=root)
519	    if active_run is None:
520	        if not is_in_task_run(slug):
521	            return GateDecision(active=False)
522	        _reject(slug, "active_run.json is missing", abort=True)
523	
524	    project_root = project_dir(slug, root=root)
525	    plan_path = project_root / "plan.json"
526	    plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(plan_path)
527	    if plan_hash != active_run["plan_hash"]:
528	        _reject(slug, "plan.json hash does not match active_run.json pin", abort=True)
529	
530	    run_id = active_run["run_id"]
531	    events_path = project_root / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
532	    ok, _last_index, error = verify_chain(events_path)
533	    if not ok:
534	        _reject(slug, error or "events.jsonl chain integrity check failed", abort=True)
535	
536	    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
537	    events = read_events(events_path)
538	    cursor = derive_cursor(plan, events, slug=slug)
539	    if cursor.pinned_failure is not None:
540	        reason, _host_path = cursor.pinned_failure
541	        raise TaskRunGateError(reason=reason, recovery=f"artagents abort --project {slug}")
542	    run_started_actor = _find_run_started_actor(events)
543	
544	    # Auto-traverse: nested_entered/exited for nested plans; iteration_started/
545	    # for_each_expanded/item_started for repeat hosts. We loop until we land on a
546	    # dispatchable leaf (CodeStep or AttestedStep) inside the appropriate frame.
547	    events_view = list(events)
548	
549	    def _gate_append(ev: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
550	        append_event(events_path, ev)
551	        events_view.append(ev)
552	
553	    leaf = _auto_traverse_to_leaf(
554	        slug=slug,
555	        cursor=cursor,
556	        events_view=events_view,
557	        incoming_command=command,
558	        project_root=project_root,
559	        run_id=run_id,
560	        append_fn=_gate_append,
561	        raise_on_exhausted=True,
562	    )
563	    if leaf is None:
564	        # Defensive: raise_on_exhausted=True should always raise inside the helper.
565	        _reject(slug, "plan is exhausted", abort=True)
566	    current_step, current_path = leaf
567	    top = cursor.frames[-1]
568	    path_str = STEP_PATH_SEP.join(current_path)
569	
570	    iteration = top.iteration if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
571	    item_id = top.item_id if top.repeat_step_id is not None else None
572	
573	    if isinstance(current_step, CodeStep):
574	        return _dispatch_code(
575	            slug=slug,
576	            command=command,
577	            step=current_step,
578	            path_str=path_str,
579	            path_tuple=current_path,
580	            events_path=events_path,
581	            run_id=run_id,
582	            reentry=reentry,
583	            project_root=project_root,
584	            iteration=iteration,
585	            item_id=item_id,
586	        )
587	    if isinstance(current_step, AttestedStep):
588	        return _dispatch_attested(
589	            slug=slug,
590	            command=command,
591	            step=current_step,
592	            path_str=path_str,
593	            path_tuple=current_path,
594	            events_path=events_path,
595	            run_id=run_id,
596	            run_started_actor=run_started_actor,
597	            project_root=project_root,
598	            iteration=iteration,
599	            item_id=item_id,
600	        )
601	    raise TaskRunGateError(
602	        reason=f"unexpected step kind: {type(current_step).__name__}",
603	        recovery=f"artagents next --project {slug}",
604	    )
605	
606	
607	def _count_iteration_failed(events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], host_path: str) -> int:
608	    return sum(
609	        1
610	        for ev in events
611	        if isinstance(ev, dict)
612	        and ev.get("kind") == "iteration_failed"
613	        and _path_str_from_event(ev) == host_path
614	    )
615	
616	
617	EXHAUST_OVERRIDE_ID = "exhaust-override"
618	
619	
620	def _has_iteration_exhausted(events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], host_path: str) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
621	    for ev in events:
622	        if (
623	            isinstance(ev, dict)
624	            and ev.get("kind") == "iteration_exhausted"
625	            and _path_str_from_event(ev) == host_path
626	        ):
627	            return ev
628	    return None
629	
630	
631	def _make_exhaust_override_step(slug: str, host_path: str) -> AttestedStep:
632	    override_path = f"{host_path}{STEP_PATH_SEP}{EXHAUST_OVERRIDE_ID}"
633	    return AttestedStep(
634	        id=EXHAUST_OVERRIDE_ID,
635	        command=f"ack --project {slug} --step {override_path}",
636	        instructions="repeat.until max_iterations exhausted; human override required to advance",
637	        ack=AckRule(kind="actor"),
638	    )
639	
640	
641	def _enter_repeat_until(
642	    *,
643	    slug: str,
644	    cursor: CursorPath,
645	    host: Any,
646	    repeat: RepeatUntil,
647	    path_str: str,
648	    parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...],
649	    events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]],
650	    append_fn: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], Any],
651	) -> None:
652	    failed = _count_iteration_failed(events, path_str)
653	    iteration = failed + 1

1096	def record_dispatch_complete(decision: GateDecision, returncode: int) -> None:
1097	    if not decision.active or decision.events_path is None or decision.plan_step_id is None:
1098	        return
1099	    if decision.step_kind == "attested":
1100	        # attested steps are advanced by step_attested itself; do not double-emit
1101	        return
1102	    append_event(
1103	        decision.events_path,
1104	        make_step_completed_event(decision.plan_step_id, returncode),
1105	    )
1106	    if decision.produces:
1107	        _run_inline_checks(decision, decision.produces)
1108	
1109	
1110	# step_dir_for_path is the ONLY directory API used in this gate path (FLAG-P3-001).
1111	def _run_inline_checks(decision: GateDecision, produces: tuple[ProducesEntry, ...]) -> bool:
1112	    if (
1113	        decision.events_path is None
1114	        or decision.run_id is None
1115	        or decision.slug is None
1116	        or decision.project_root is None
1117	        or not decision.plan_step_path
1118	    ):
1119	        return True
1120	    projects_root = decision.project_root.parent
1121	    step_dir = step_dir_for_path(
1122	        decision.slug,
1123	        decision.run_id,
1124	        decision.plan_step_path,
1125	        iteration=decision.iteration,
1126	        item_id=decision.item_id,
1127	        root=projects_root,
1128	    )
1129	    for entry in produces:
1130	        artifact_path = step_dir / entry.path
1131	        result = entry.check.run(artifact_path)
1132	        if not result.ok:
1133	            append_event(
1134	                decision.events_path,
1135	                make_produces_check_failed_event(
1136	                    decision.plan_step_path,
1137	                    entry.name,
1138	                    check_id=entry.check.check_id,
1139	                    reason=result.reason,
1140	                ),
1141	            )
1142	            if decision.iteration is not None:

1144	                    decision.events_path,
1145	                    make_iteration_failed_event(
1146	                        decision.plan_step_path,
1147	                        decision.iteration,
1148	                        reason=f"produces check failed: {entry.name}",
1149	                    ),
1150	                )
1151	            else:
1152	                append_event(
1153	                    decision.events_path,
1154	                    make_cursor_rewind_event(
1155	                        decision.plan_step_path,
1156	                        reason=f"produces check failed: {entry.name}",
1157	                    ),
1158	                )
1159	            return False
1160	        cas_sha256 = _intern_produces_artifact(decision, artifact_path)
1161	        append_event(
1162	            decision.events_path,
1163	            make_produces_check_passed_event(
1164	                decision.plan_step_path,
1165	                entry.name,
1166	                check_id=entry.check.check_id,
1167	                cas_sha256=cas_sha256,
1168	            ),
1169	        )
1170	    return True

1173	def _intern_produces_artifact(decision: GateDecision, artifact_path: Path) -> str | None:
1174	    if decision.project_root is None:
1175	        return None
1176	    if artifact_path.is_symlink():
1177	        return None
1178	    cas_target = intern(decision.project_root, artifact_path)
1179	    link_into_produces(cas_target, artifact_path)
1180	    return cas_target.name

"""Shared helpers for Phase 5 lifecycle tests (T12-T20).

Not a test module (doesn't match test_*.py glob). Provides:
- ``make_pack``: write a tiny DSL pack module under packs/<pack>/<name>.py
- ``setup_packs_and_compile``: build packs root + compile a qualified id to JSON
- ``setup_run``: build packs + projects roots, compile, and call cmd_start
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import io
import os
from contextlib import redirect_stdout
from pathlib import Path

from artagents.core.task.lifecycle import cmd_start
from artagents.orchestrate.compile import compile_to_path


def make_pack(packs_root: Path, pack: str, module_name: str, body: str) -> Path:
    pack_dir = packs_root / pack
    pack_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    module_path = pack_dir / f"{module_name}.py"
    module_path.write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
    return module_path


def setup_packs_and_compile(
    tmp_path: Path,
    pack: str,
    module_name: str,
    body: str,
    qualified_id: str,
) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Returns (packs_root, projects_root). Compiles the qualified id."""
    packs = tmp_path / "packs"
    projects = tmp_path / "projects"
    packs.mkdir()
    projects.mkdir()
    make_pack(packs, pack, module_name, body)
    compile_to_path(qualified_id, packs_root=packs)
    return packs, projects


def setup_run(
    tmp_path: Path,
    pack: str,
    module_name: str,
    body: str,
    qualified_id: str,
    *,
    run_id: str,
    project: str = "p",
    start_actor: str = "bob",
) -> tuple[Path, Path]:
    """Compile + cmd_start. Returns (packs_root, projects_root). Sets
    ARTAGENTS_ACTOR=start_actor before cmd_start so run_started.actor==start_actor.
    Caller is responsible for adjusting ARTAGENTS_ACTOR before subsequent ack calls.
    """
    packs, projects = setup_packs_and_compile(tmp_path, pack, module_name, body, qualified_id)
    os.environ["ARTAGENTS_ACTOR"] = start_actor
    with redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()):
        rc = cmd_start(
            [qualified_id, "--project", project, "--name", run_id],
            packs_root=packs,
            projects_root=projects,
        )
    if rc != 0:
        raise RuntimeError(f"setup_run: cmd_start rc={rc}")
    return packs, projects
```

4	
5	import os
6	from collections.abc import Mapping
7	
8	TASK_RUN_ID_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_TASK_RUN_ID"
9	TASK_PROJECT_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_TASK_PROJECT"
10	TASK_STEP_ID_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_TASK_STEP_ID"
11	TASK_ITEM_ID_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_TASK_ITEM_ID"
12	TASK_ITERATION_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_TASK_ITERATION"
13	ARTAGENTS_ACTOR = "ARTAGENTS_ACTOR"

15	
16	def task_project_env() -> str | None:
17	    return os.environ.get(TASK_PROJECT_ENV)
18	
19	
20	def task_run_id_env() -> str | None:
21	    return os.environ.get(TASK_RUN_ID_ENV)
22	
23	
24	def task_step_id_env() -> str | None:
25	    return os.environ.get(TASK_STEP_ID_ENV)

29	    return os.environ.get(TASK_ITEM_ID_ENV)

31

33	    return os.environ.get(TASK_ITERATION_ENV)
34	
35

40	def is_in_task_run(slug: str | None = None) -> bool:
41	    run_id = task_run_id_env()
42	    if not run_id:
43	        return False
44	    return slug is None or task_project_env() == slug
45	
46	
47	def apply_task_run_env(
48	    run_id: str,
49	    project_slug: str,
50	    step_id: str,
51	    *,
52	    item_id: str | None = None,
53	    iteration: int | None = None,
54	) -> None:
55	    os.environ[TASK_RUN_ID_ENV] = run_id
56	    os.environ[TASK_PROJECT_ENV] = project_slug
57	    os.environ[TASK_STEP_ID_ENV] = step_id
58	    if item_id is None:
59	        os.environ.pop(TASK_ITEM_ID_ENV, None)
60	    else:
61	        os.environ[TASK_ITEM_ID_ENV] = item_id
62	    if iteration is None:
63	        os.environ.pop(TASK_ITERATION_ENV, None)
64	    else:
65	        os.environ[TASK_ITERATION_ENV] = f"{int(iteration):03d}"
66	
67	
68	def child_subprocess_env(*, base: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
69	    env = dict(os.environ if base is None else base)
70	    env.pop(ARTAGENTS_ACTOR, None)
71	    for key in (
72	        TASK_RUN_ID_ENV,
73	        TASK_PROJECT_ENV,
74	        TASK_STEP_ID_ENV,
75	        TASK_ITEM_ID_ENV,
76	        TASK_ITERATION_ENV,
77	    ):
78	        value = os.environ.get(key)
79	        if value is not None:
80	            env[key] = value
81	    return env
82

128	def derive_cursor(plan: TaskPlan, events: Sequence[dict[str, Any]], *, slug: str = "") -> CursorPath:
129	    """Replay ``events.jsonl`` left-to-right to reconstruct the path-stack cursor.
130	
131	    Reconstructible from events alone (supports partial-replay resume):
132	    ``nested_entered`` pushes, ``nested_exited`` pops + advances parent,
133	    ``step_completed`` / ``step_attested`` mark a step advance-eligible iff it
134	    has no produces; otherwise advance is deferred until the contiguous block
135	    contains a ``produces_check_passed`` for every declared produces name.
136	    ``produces_check_failed`` / ``cursor_rewind`` clear pending state without
137	    advancing. ``iteration_started`` pushes an iteration frame; ``iteration_failed``
138	    pops it without advancing the host. ``for_each_expanded`` records the host's
139	    item set on the cursor (used as the source of truth — derive_cursor never
140	    re-reads disk during replay). ``item_started`` pushes a per-item frame;
141	    ``item_completed`` / ``item_attested`` pop the item frame and advance the
142	    host once every item is done. ``step_dispatched`` / ``run_started`` do not
143	    advance.
144	    """
145	    frames: list[_Frame] = [_Frame(plan=plan, path_prefix=(), child_index=0)]
146	    pending: list[set[str] | None] = [None]
147	    for_each_progress: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
148	    pinned_failure: tuple[str, str] | None = None
149	    for event in events:
150	        kind = event.get("kind")
151	        if kind == "iteration_exhausted":
152	            on_exhaust = event.get("on_exhaust")
153	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
154	            if on_exhaust == "fail":
155	                pinned_failure = ("repeat.until max_iterations exhausted", host_path)
156	                continue
157	            if on_exhaust == "escalate":
158	                top = frames[-1]
159	                if top.child_index < len(top.plan.steps):
160	                    host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
161	                    override_plan = TaskPlan(
162	                        plan_id=f"__exhaust_{host_step.id}",
163	                        version=1,
164	                        steps=(_make_exhaust_override_step(slug, host_path),),
165	                    )
166	                    frames.append(
167	                        _Frame(
168	                            plan=override_plan,
169	                            path_prefix=top.path_prefix + (host_step.id,),
170	                            child_index=0,
171	                            repeat_step_id=host_step.id,
172	                        )
173	                    )
174	                    pending.append(None)
175	            continue
176	        if kind == "nested_entered":
177	            top = frames[-1]
178	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
179	                raise TaskRunGateError(
180	                    reason="nested_entered points past end of frame",
181	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
182	                )
183	            step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
184	            if not isinstance(step, NestedStep):
185	                raise TaskRunGateError(
186	                    reason="nested_entered did not land on a NestedStep",
187	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
188	                )
189	            frames.append(
190	                _Frame(
191	                    plan=step.plan,
192	                    path_prefix=top.path_prefix + (step.id,),
193	                    child_index=0,
194	                )
195	            )
196	            pending.append(None)
197	        elif kind == "nested_exited":
198	            if len(frames) <= 1:
199	                raise TaskRunGateError(
200	                    reason="nested_exited at root frame",
201	                    recovery="inspect events.jsonl",
202	                )
203	            frames.pop()
204	            pending.pop()
205	            frames[-1].child_index += 1
206	            pending[-1] = None
207	        elif kind == "iteration_started":
208	            top = frames[-1]
209	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
210	                continue
211	            host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
212	            iteration = int(event.get("iteration", 1))
213	            frames.append(_make_iteration_frame(host_step, top.path_prefix, iteration))
214	            pending.append(None)
215	        elif kind == "iteration_failed":
216	            if frames[-1].repeat_step_id is None:
217	                continue
218	            frames.pop()
219	            pending.pop()
220	            pending[-1] = None
221	        elif kind == "for_each_expanded":
222	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
223	            items = tuple(event.get("item_ids") or ())
224	            for_each_progress.setdefault(host_path, {"items": items, "completed": set()})
225	            for_each_progress[host_path]["items"] = items
226	        elif kind == "item_started":
227	            top = frames[-1]
228	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
229	                continue
230	            host_step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
231	            item_id = event.get("item_id")
232	            if not isinstance(item_id, str):
233	                continue
234	            frames.append(_make_item_frame(host_step, top.path_prefix, item_id))
235	            pending.append(None)
236	        elif kind in ("item_completed", "item_attested"):
237	            host_path = _path_str_from_event(event)
238	            item_id = event.get("item_id")
239	            if not isinstance(item_id, str):
240	                continue
241	            entry = for_each_progress.setdefault(host_path, {"items": (), "completed": set()})
242	            entry["completed"].add(item_id)
243	            if frames[-1].item_id == item_id:
244	                frames.pop()
245	                pending.pop()
246	                pending[-1] = None
247	            # If all items now completed and the host's parent frame is on top, advance host.
248	            if entry["items"] and set(entry["items"]) <= entry["completed"]:
249	                host_segments = host_path.split(STEP_PATH_SEP) if host_path else []
250	                expected_parent_prefix = tuple(host_segments[:-1])
251	                if tuple(frames[-1].path_prefix) == expected_parent_prefix:
252	                    if frames[-1].child_index < len(frames[-1].plan.steps):
253	                        candidate = frames[-1].plan.steps[frames[-1].child_index]
254	                        if host_segments and candidate.id == host_segments[-1]:
255	                            frames[-1].child_index += 1
256	                            pending[-1] = None
257	        elif kind in ("step_completed", "step_attested"):
258	            top = frames[-1]
259	            if top.child_index >= len(top.plan.steps):
260	                continue
261	            step = top.plan.steps[top.child_index]
262	            produces = getattr(step, "produces", ())
263	            if not produces:
264	                top.child_index += 1
265	                pending[-1] = None
266	            else:
267	                pending[-1] = {entry.name for entry in produces}
268	        elif kind == "produces_check_passed":
269	            current = pending[-1]
270	            if current is not None:
271	                name = event.get("produces_name")
272	                current.discard(name)
273	                if not current:
274	                    frames[-1].child_index += 1
275	                    pending[-1] = None
276	        elif kind in ("produces_check_failed", "cursor_rewind"):
277	            pending[-1] = None
278	        elif kind == "step_dispatched":
279	            pending[-1] = None
280	        # run_started / iteration_exhausted: no-op for cursor
281	    _finalize_cursor(frames, pending, for_each_progress)
282	    return CursorPath(frames=frames, for_each_progress=for_each_progress, pinned_failure=pinned_failure)
283	
284	
285	def _make_iteration_frame(host_step: Any, parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...], iteration: int) -> _Frame:
286	    body = dataclasses.replace(host_step, repeat=None) if hasattr(host_step, "repeat") else host_step
287	    body_plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=f"__iter_{host_step.id}_{iteration}", version=1, steps=(body,))
288	    return _Frame(
289	        plan=body_plan,
290	        path_prefix=parent_prefix,
291	        child_index=0,
292	        iteration=iteration,
293	        repeat_step_id=host_step.id,
294	    )
295	
296	
297	def _make_item_frame(host_step: Any, parent_prefix: tuple[str, ...], item_id: str) -> _Frame:
298	    body = dataclasses.replace(host_step, repeat=None) if hasattr(host_step, "repeat") else host_step
299	    body_plan = TaskPlan(plan_id=f"__item_{host_step.id}_{item_id}", version=1, steps=(body,))
300	    return _Frame(
301	        plan=body_plan,
302	        path_prefix=parent_prefix,
303	        child_index=0,
304	        item_id=item_id,
305	        repeat_step_id=host_step.id,
306	    )
307	
308	
309	def _finalize_cursor(
310	    frames: list[_Frame],
311	    pending: list[set[str] | None],
312	    for_each_progress: dict[str, dict[str, Any]],
313	) -> None:
314	    """Pop exhausted iteration/item frames after event replay.
315	
316	    For verifier_passes / approve cases, the iteration frame ends with
317	    ``produces_check_passed`` coverage or ``step_attested`` (no following
318	    iteration_failed) — the iter frame's child_index is at end-of-plan.
319	    Pop it and advance the host. Same for item frames whose item is in
320	    the for_each_progress.completed set.
321	    """
322	    while True:
323	        top = frames[-1]
324	        if top.repeat_step_id is None:
325	            break
326	        if top.child_index < len(top.plan.steps):
327	            break

The plan calls `consume_inbox_entry` -> synthesizes `peek.step.command + identity tokens` -> `gate_command` (i.e. an approve). It does not say what happens for `decision: 'retry'` (which the existing `cmd_ack` only allows when the latest event is `produces_check_failed`) or `decision: 'abort'` (which delegates to `cmd_abort`). At minimum the plan should explicitly reject or document handling of non-approve decisions; otherwise the brief's own format is partially unimplemented.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"Brief defines `evidence` as a JSON object/dict (`evidence: { ... step-specific artifacts ... }`), but the gate's attested path takes evidence as a tuple of strings (paths) — see `AttestedArgs.evidence: tuple[str, ...]` in gate.py:97 and `_ack_approve` building `parts += ['--evidence', ev]` in lifecycle_ack.py:210. The plan stores `evidence: dict[str, Any]` on `InboxEntry` but never says how a dict is translated into the string tokens that gate_command/_match_attested_command expect. Either the file format must change to a list-of-strings, or `consume_inbox_entry` must serialize evidence differently — neither is specified.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"Brief: 'Stale or malformed files are ignored (logged but do not crash).' Plan correctly logs+skips malformed in `scan_inbox`, and stale step-mismatch entries are 'logged, not consumed' — but the plan leaves stale files in `inbox/` indefinitely. They will be re-logged on every `next`/`status` call AND counted as 'pending' in cmd_status output forever. Brief does not require quarantine, but the plan's status surface (`X inbox entries pending`) will become misleading once any stale entry exists.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"AGENT.md insertion location is internally inconsistent. Step 6 prose says 'add a note about the inbox surface under the RECOVERY COMMANDS section', but the explicit instruction immediately below says 'append to `_AGENT_MD_TEMPLATE` after line 68'. Line 68 in lifecycle.py is the closing `\\\"\\\"\\\"` of the STOP HOOK paragraph, not the RECOVERY COMMANDS section. The reviewer/executor must guess which one is intended.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"Brief: 'Inbox files validate into events on the next next/status/ack call.' Plan explicitly says cmd_status surfaces a count but does NOT consume — and `artagents ack` is also untouched (the plan calls this out). That contradicts the brief's literal wording (which lists status and ack as consumption points). The plan's stance is defensible (status is read-only; ack is an explicit verb), but it is a deviation from the brief that should be acknowledged.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        }\n      ]\n    },\n    {\n      \"id\": \"correctness\",\n      \"question\": \"Are the proposed changes technically correct?\",\n      \"guidance\": \"Look for logic errors, invalid assumptions, broken invariants, schema mismatches, or behavior that would fail at runtime. When the fix adds a conditional branch, check whether it handles all relevant cases — not just the one reported in the issue.\",\n      \"findings\": [\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"Identity validation will reject inbox-driven approves under the proposed `gate_command` reuse. `_dispatch_attested` calls `validate_attested_identity` (gate.py:1064), which for `ack.kind=='actor'` requires `task_actor_env() == args.actor` (gate.py:1082) and rejects self-ack when `run_started_actor == args.actor == task_actor_env()` (gate.py:1087-1092). The inbox is dropped by an external system; when `cmd_next` consumes it, `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` will either be unset or hold the operator's name — neither equals `submitted_by`. The plan does not say to set/spoof the env, nor does it bypass identity checks. As written, every actor-kind inbox approve will fail with 'attested --actor does not match ARTAGENTS_ACTOR'. This is the central correctness issue and is unaddressed.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"InboxEntry has only `submitted_by: str` but the plan's synthesized command must choose either `--agent <id>` or `--actor <name>` based on `step.ack.kind`. The plan does not document this mapping. If the executor blindly matches `submitted_by` to `ack.kind`, an inbox file with the wrong kind (e.g., human submitting where the step expects an agent) will be rejected silently. The file format itself should probably carry the kind, or the plan should explicitly state 'submitted_by is treated as ack.kind dictates'.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"Plan has no handling for the case where the cursor is on a CodeStep or NestedStep but the inbox file references it. `consume_inbox_entry` is described as validating 'against the current step expectations' and returning False on mismatch — but the plan doesn't say the kind must be `AttestedStep`. Plan metadata assumption #3 says 'Only attested steps can be completed via inbox' but the implementation step (Step 1) doesn't enforce this guard. Without an explicit `isinstance(peek.step, AttestedStep)` check, calling `gate_command` against a code step's command would either dispatch the code step or hit `command != step.command` rejection paths.\",\n          \"flagged\": true\n        },\n        {\n          \"detail\": \"Step 4 inserts inbox consumption between `events = read_events(events_path)` and `peek = peek_current_step(...)`, then re-reads `events` after consumption. But if `consume_pending_inbox` advanced the cursor (via gate_command emitting step_attested), the re-read events feed peek_current_step correctly only if the for_each/iteration ledger views also rebuild from the new events.
